Abstract: Simulating deformable objects is essential for a wide range of robotic manipulation applications, yet accurately predicting their dynamics remains challenging. We propose Physics-Guided Residual Dynamics (PGRD), a hybrid simulation framework that combines the advantages of physics-based and learning-based approaches. Specifically, PGRD combines an optimizable spring-mass simulator as a backbone with a learned neural network that predicts residual corrections to the physics-based predictions. We adopt a velocity-based formulation to ensure stable simulation and a sliding-window transformer architecture to capture temporal dependencies. We show that PGRD produces more accurate results than both purely physics-based and learning-based methods on a set of diverse real-world deformable objects. We further demonstrate the utility of PGRD in two applications: manipulation planning via Model Predictive Control, including a language-conditioned setting with a generated goal image; and interactive simulation via action-conditioned video prediction by 3D Gaussian Splatting.

</details>


### [106] [Reverse to Advance: Teleoperation-Cost Effective Hard Policy Learning from Reversed Easy Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13455)
*Qiyuan Qiao,Ge Yuan,Can Wang,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过逆向简单任务轨迹来低成本获取复杂任务演示数据的框架（TELeR），包含自动采集、分层精化、迭代学习三组件，在模拟和真实机器人上提升了高精度操作任务的成功率，减少了对困难任务遥操作数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量遥操作数据集成本高昂，尤其对于困难任务。观察到许多任务存在方向不对称性：正向困难任务难以完成，而反向（放松/破坏环境）相对容易，因此逆向简单任务轨迹可作为困难任务的低成本监督信号。

Method: 提出TELeR框架：(1) 闭环数据采集流水线，交替执行困难/简单任务策略自动重置环境生成多样化轨迹；(2) 分层数据精化流水线，时序反转简单任务轨迹，使用运动学先验和critic引导优势滤波器过滤低质量运动；(3) 迭代策略学习方法，使用初始逆向演示和过滤后的逆向数据持续在线学习。

Result: 在两个模拟基准和真实机器人实验中，相比基于反转的基线方法和强化学习方法，该方法以更高数据效率和更稳定训练提升了困难任务成功率，且无需大量困难任务遥操作。

Conclusion: 通过自动采集、分层精化与迭代学习的结合，实现了可扩展、可靠的复杂高精度操作任务训练，为降低遥操作数据成本提供了有效方案。

Abstract: High-quality teleoperation datasets are costly to collect, particularly for hard tasks. We observe that many tasks exhibit directional asymmetry: completing the forward hard task is difficult, whereas reversing it by relaxing or disrupting the environment is comparatively easy. This suggests that reversed easy-task trajectories can serve as a scalable supervision signal for the hard task, reducing the cost of manual demonstration collection. However, reversed data can be noisy, and directly training on it may yield suboptimal policies. To enable largely automated acquisition and effective use of reversed data, we propose a teleoperation-cost effective framework for hard policy learning via temporal reversal of easy tasks, consisting of three key components: a closed-loop data collection pipeline that alternates between hard-task and easy-task policies to autonomously reset the environment and generate diverse trajectories; a hierarchical data refinement pipeline that temporally inverts easy-task rollouts and filters low-quality motion using kinematic priors and a critic-guided advantage filter; and an iterative policy learning method that trains the hard-task policy using both initial reversed easy-task demonstrations and the filtered reversed data in a continuous online learning loop. By combining automated collection, hierarchical refinement, and iterative learning, our method enables scalable, reliable training of complex, high-precision manipulation tasks. Across two simulated benchmarks and real-robot experiments, we demonstrate that our method improves hard-task success rates with higher data efficiency and more stable training compared to reversal-based and reinforcement-learning baselines, without requiring extensive hard-task teleoperation.

</details>


### [107] [Joint On-and-Off Policy Learning for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13461)
*Qingrong He,Lin Zhao,Kevin Zheng,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JOP-VLN通过三阶段训练流程将离线模仿学习与在线探索策略协同结合，在VLN-CE基准上达到SOTA，R2R成功率达69.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的VLN方法存在两个独立范式——基于专家示范的模仿学习（IL+DAgger）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RL），缺乏两者融合的框架，限制了导航智能体的误差恢复和探索能力。

Method: 三阶段训练：阶段1用IL从专家示范学习基础导航技能；阶段2用DAgger算法生成启发式探索轨迹并用于IL以提升误差恢复；阶段3提出联合在线-离线策略学习，包含高熵轨迹采样提升RL训练效率，以及误差纠正优先的轨迹排序策略。

Result: R2R基准上SR 69.9%，RxR基准上SR 68.0%，在R2R上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 离线IL与在线RL的联合训练范式有效提升了VLN性能，为VLM-based导航方法提供了新的融合框架。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) necessitates an embodied agent to navigate in the physical world by adhering to natural language instructions. Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLM) have propelled the development of VLM-based VLN methods with two predominant paradigms: (1) imitation learning (IL) on expert demonstrations, followed by the Dataset Aggregation (DAgger) algorithm to bolster error recovery capabilities; (2) reinforcement learning (RL) driven by verifiable rewards to enhance reasoning and exploration. A notable gap is the absence of integration between these two distinct paradigms. This paper introduces JOP-VLN, a novel VLN framework that synergistically combines off-policy imitation learning and on-policy exploration within a three-stage training pipeline. Initially, IL is employed on expert demonstrations to acquire basic navigation skills. Subsequently, the DAgger algorithm is utilized to generate heuristic exploration trajectories, which are then used for imitation learning to improve error recovery capabilities. Finally, a joint on-and-off policy learning framework is implemented, featuring high-entropy trajectory sampling to enhance RL training efficiency and an error-correction-prioritized trajectory sorting strategy for effective error correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of JOP-VLN, achieving success rates of 69.9% and 68.0% on the VLN-CE R2R and RxR benchmarks, respectively, setting a new state-of-the-art on R2R. Project page: https://qingrongh.github.io/JOP-VLN.

</details>


### [108] [EgoHTR: Egocentric 4D Demonstrations of Human Terrain Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13472)
*Alex Brandes,Haig Conti Georges Sajelian,Manthan Patel,Dominik Hollidt,Chenhao Li,Matthias Heyrman,Oliver Hausdoerfer,Manuel Kaufmann,Xi Wang,Jonas Frey,Angela P. Schoellig,Christian Holz,Marc Pollefeys,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoHTR数据集，基于第一人称穿戴设备和便携3D扫描仪构建55段场景对齐的4D人体运动序列（15万帧），用于复杂地形下人形机器人的感知运动策略训练，并在Unitree G1上完成硬件部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习难以处理复杂地形下的人形机器人部署，而利用人类先验的方法缺乏上下文感知能力，根源在于现有数据管线无法捕获挑战性环境中的人-场景序列。

Method: 开发多传感器融合重建管线（第一人称穿戴设备+便携3D扫描仪），采集55段场景对齐的4D人体运动序列（15万帧），并基于此数据训练感知运动策略。

Result: 数据集精度达到SOTA（与动捕真值对比），在Unitree G1上实现硬件部署，基于重建参考运动完成真实地形行走。

Conclusion: 提供社区可扩展的数据集管线，将问题分解以帮助构建上下文感知的、能在不平坦地形上可靠行走的基础人形机器人。

Abstract: Deploying humanoid robots in unstructured terrain remains an open problem. While classic reinforcement learning struggles with the sheer complexity of real-world interactions, more promising methods leveraging human priors remain limited to models lacking contextual awareness. The restricted motion synthesis is a direct consequence of existing dataset pipelines failing to capture human-scene sequences in challenging environments. To bridge this gap between humanoid learning and scene reconstruction, we introduce the Egocentric Human-Terrain Reconstruction (EgoHTR) dataset. We develop and open-source a reconstruction pipeline capturing 55 scene-aligned 4D human motion sequences in diverse, complex environments using a multi-sensor setup of egocentric wearables and a portable 3D scanner. The resulting dataset comprises over 150k frames, which we evaluate against motion-capture ground truth, demonstrating state-of-the-art accuracy and establishing a rigorous benchmark for human motion analysis and synthesis. Further, we leverage this data to train perceptive locomotion policies, demonstrating hardware deployment on a Unitree G1 for reconstructed reference motions. Our pipeline enables community-driven dataset extensions and factors the problem to help researchers build foundational, context-aware robots that reliably traverse uneven terrain.

</details>


### [109] [Deformable State Estimation for Autonomous Surgical Tissue Retraction Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13475)
*Everest Yang,Skye Thompson,George D. Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的状态估计器，利用多层感知机和PCA低维潜在表示，从40个稀疏噪声顶点观测中重构可变形组织网格的完整状态，在2D可变形薄片仿真中实现多步牵拉规划中98.1%的oracle性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手术组织牵拉中，感知部分且含噪声，现有方法难以从稀疏观测中有效估计可变形组织的完整状态，亟需一种能高效推理且物理合理的学习型状态估计方法。

Method: 结合MLP与PCA低维潜在表示构建状态估计器，使用几何感知正则化（鼓励平滑且物理合理的变形）训练，输入40个噪声顶点观测，输出完整变形网格状态。

Result: 在2D可变形薄片仿真中，多步牵拉规划达到oracle性能的98.1%，支持高效推理。

Conclusion: 几何正则化的学习型状态估计能在现实感知约束下支持有效的可变形操作。

Abstract: Surgical tissue retraction requires effective manipulation planning under partial and noisy perception. We study state estimation for deformable tissue retraction, where only sparse observations of the tissue surface are available at decision time. We propose a learned state estimator that reconstructs the full deformable mesh state from 40 noisy vertex observations. The estimator combines a multilayer perceptron with a low-dimensional PCA latent representation and is trained using geometry-aware regularization that encourages smooth and physically plausible deformations. We evaluate the approach in a 2D deformable sheet simulation using single-step and multi-step retraction planning. Results show that the learned estimator achieves 98.1% of oracle performance in multi-step retraction while supporting efficient inference. These results demonstrate that learned, geometry-regularized state estimation can support effective deformable manipulation under realistic perception constraints.

</details>


### [110] [VAMP-MR: Vector-Accelerated Motion Planning and Execution for Multi-Robot-Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13478)
*Philip Huang,Chenrui Gao,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一套结合经典规划算法与SIMD向量化碰撞检测技术的多机械臂运动规划器，在工业多臂操作任务中实现了近实时运动生成，加速比达两个数量级，并开源了代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于搜索和采样的多机械臂运动规划器计算耗时严重，难以产生高质量、无碰撞的运动以满足安全实时的工业执行需求。

Method: 将经典规划算法与基于CPU SIMD指令的最先进向量化碰撞检测技术结合，加速规划中的主要瓶颈——碰撞检测环节。

Result: 在多臂操作任务中，运动规划和执行后处理实现了最高两个数量级的加速，达到近实时性能。

Conclusion: 通过SIMD向量化加速碰撞检测，能显著提升多机械臂运动规划的效率，开源实现有助于降低该领域的研究门槛。

Abstract: Multi-robot-arm motion planning is a key challenge in deploying multiple manipulators for industrial tasks such as manufacturing. Existing search-based and sampling-based solvers often require significant computation time to produce collision-free, high-quality motions suitable for safe real-world execution. In this work, we introduce a new suite of multi-robot-arm motion planners capable of near real-time motion generation, combining classical planning algorithms with state-of-the-art vectorized collision-checking techniques. Based on CPU SIMD instructions, our new planners accelerate their primary bottleneck, collision checking, and achieve up to two orders of magnitude speedup in both motion planning and execution postprocessing for multi-arm manipulation tasks. We also release our implementation to lower the barrier for research and development of multi-robot-arm planning and manipulation problems. Code is available at https://vamp-mr.github.io/vamp-mr

</details>


### [111] [Topology-Agnostic Mesh Reconstruction of Deformable Objects from Sparse Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13479)
*Everest Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅通过少量触觉（无视觉）即可重建可变形物体完整网格的拓扑无关估计器，使用排列不变交叉注意力架构统一处理1D绳索、2D布料和3D软体，将重建误差降低约三分之二，并利用深度集成不确定性指导主动触摸策略进一步降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉不可用（黑暗、遮挡、袋内等）情况下，可变形物体的完整形状估计极具挑战，触觉是天然传感方式但稀疏且局部，亟需一种仅依赖少量触觉即可进行高质量完整形状重建的方法。

Method: 使用单一拓扑无关的排列不变交叉注意力架构，将稀疏触觉点作为输入，直接回归输出完整网格；采用深度集成估计不确定性指导下一个触摸点的主动选择策略。

Result: 相比非学习几何补全和高斯过程基线，重建误差降低约三分之二；优于全局池化集合编码器，且差距随触摸点增多而扩大；主动触摸策略在稀疏预算下优于随机触摸和高斯过程主动基线，在自遮挡严重时增益更明显；当视觉可用时触摸位置选择不再重要。

Conclusion: 仅靠少量触觉即可高质量重建可变形物体完整形状，主动感知策略在视觉缺失场景下有实际价值，为触觉驱动的形状重建提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Estimating the full shape of a deformable object is especially challenging when vision is unavailable: in the dark, inside an opaque bag, behind the manipulating hand, or under heavy self-occlusion. Touch is the natural sensor in these settings, but touches are sparse and local. We present a single topology-agnostic estimator that reconstructs the full mesh of a deformable object from only a few touches and no vision, using one permutation-invariant cross-attention architecture that handles a 1D rope, a 2D cloth, and a 3D volumetric soft body. The learned estimator reduces reconstruction error by roughly two-thirds relative to non-learned geometric mesh completion and a Gaussian-process surface baseline, and it outperforms a simpler global-pool set encoder, with the gap growing as more touches are observed. We then show that the estimator's deep-ensemble uncertainty can be used to learn where to touch next, which lowers error further and beats both random touching and a Gaussian-process active baseline at sparse budgets. This gain is modest on average but grows with self-occlusion and on the error tail. When vision is also available, where to touch barely matters, motivating the vision-free setting we study.

</details>


### [112] [Layered Risk Mapping for Autonomous Patient Transport in Expeditionary Medical Facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13497)
*Lorena Maria Genua,Sarvesh Prajapati,Damla Leblebicioglu,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对远征医疗设施中高风险、高消耗的患者转运问题，提出了一种基于Noisy-OR融合的层次化风险地图框架，将地形坡度、静态障碍物、动态障碍物和语义可通行性四类异构环境风险统一为概率代价面，在蒙特卡洛仿真中将碰撞率从73%降至32%以下，障碍物间隙提升两倍以上，并在真实电动轮椅上完成室内外三种任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远征医疗设施中患者常规转运消耗大量个人防护装备、占用医护人员、且增加感染风险，尤其在激增条件下不可持续。现有自主轮椅技术无法应对高度非结构化和动态环境中的安全关键性导航挑战。

Method: 提出层次化风险地图框架，通过Noisy-OR融合模型将四类异构环境风险（地形坡度、静态障碍物、动态障碍物、语义可通行性）统一为概率代价面，用于路径规划中的风险感知决策。

Result: 蒙特卡洛评估中碰撞率从73%降至32%以下，障碍物间隙提升两倍以上；Noisy-OR在所有测试风险密度下获得最高障碍物间隙和最低条件峰值风险；真实电动轮椅在室内外三种任务剖面中成功验证。

Conclusion: 该架构成功满足了此前未被解决的远征医疗设施中自主安全导航的规划需求，为高风险动态环境下的安全患者转运提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In expeditionary medical facilities, routine patient transport imposes a compounding burden of personal protective equipment consumption, staff diversion, and elevated infection risk that becomes unsustainable under surge conditions. While autonomous wheelchairs could absorb this operational load, the safety-critical nature of patient transit within these highly unstructured and dynamic environments poses complex navigational challenges. To address this, we present a layered risk mapping framework that fuses four heterogeneous environmental hazards (terrain slope, static and dynamic obstacles, and semantic traversability) into a unified probabilistic cost surface via a Noisy-OR fusion model. In a paired Monte-Carlo evaluation, risk-informed fusion reduces collision rates from over 73% to under 32% and more than doubles obstacle clearance relative to a risk-unaware baseline. Additionaly, Noisy-OR achieves the highest clearance to obstacles and the lowest conditional peak risk across all tested hazard densities. We further validate the framework on a commercial powered wheelchair across three representative mission profiles in indoor and outdoor deployments, demonstrating that this architecture successfully meets the planning requirements of this previously unaddressed operational regime.

</details>


### [113] [Improving Map Consistency in Graph-Based LiDAR SLAM Through Information-Aware Odometry and Retroactive Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13516)
*Saurabh Gupta,Niklas Trekel,Louis Wiesmann,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一套结合不确定性感知的ICP里程计与分层回环检测的3D LiDAR SLAM框架，通过估计几何相关的信息矩阵对里程计约束进行加权，并利用层次化回环模块和回溯回环检测来提升回访位置的局部地图一致性，在多个数据集上实现了与SOTA相当或更优的全局轨迹精度和更好的局部地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图优化LiDAR SLAM系统虽然轨迹精度较好，但低轨迹误差并不能保证地图的几何一致性，尤其是在回访区域，遗漏的回环和残余漂移会导致局部地图错位。需要一种能联合优化全局轨迹估计和局部地图质量的方法。

Method: 1) 提出高效估计ICP几何相关信息矩阵的框架，用于对位姿图中里程计约束进行原则性加权；2) 引入分层回环模块，将位置识别与几何配准解耦；3) 引入回溯回环模块，利用优化后的位姿图恢复遗漏的回环；4) 提出用于评估回访位置地图一致性的评价协议。

Result: 在多个数据集上，全局轨迹精度与SOTA相当或更优，同时在回访位置的地图几何一致性上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将不确定性感知的里程计与几何引导的回环精化相结合，能够产生更精确的轨迹和更高质量的地图。

Abstract: High-quality maps are fundamental for robotics tasks such as navigation and planning. Although modern graph-based LiDAR SLAM systems achieve good trajectory accuracies, a low trajectory error alone does not guarantee geometrically consistent maps, particularly at revisit locations where missed loop closures and residual drift can produce local misalignments. In this work, we address the problem of jointly improving global trajectory estimation and local map quality in 3D LiDAR SLAM. We first propose a framework to efficiently estimate geometry-dependent information matrices for ICP, enabling principled weighting of odometry constraints in a pose graph. We then introduce a hierarchical loop-closure module that decouples place recognition from geometric registration, together with a retroactive loop-closure module that exploits the optimized pose graph to recover missed loop closures. We also propose an evaluation protocol to measure map consistency at revisit locations. We evaluate our SLAM system on several datasets against state-of-the-art LiDAR SLAM systems. Experimental results demonstrate global trajectory accuracies on par with or better than existing methods while consistently improving local geometric map consistency at revisit locations. These results suggest that coupling uncertainty-aware odometry with geometry-guided loop-closure refinement leads to more accurate trajectories and higher-quality maps.

</details>


### [114] [Kepler-Encoder-v0.1: Towards a Multimodal Embedding Model for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13522)
*Ishneet Sukhvinder Singh,Dhanoosh Pooranakumaran,Alex Nguyen,Jia Qi Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Kepler-Encoder-v0.1，一种以机器人为中心的多模态编码器，在训练时融合视觉、本体感知和力/扭矩，但推理时仅用视觉，通过掩码跨模态预测自监督学习。实验证明其视觉潜变量能恢复视觉本身难以捕捉的力/接触信息，并可用于无训练的状态异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机只能看到机器人身体的一部分，力和接触在单帧图像中几乎没有痕迹，原始视觉特征对力的预测R²低于0.10。需要一种方法让视觉编码器在推理时仅靠图像就能隐式携带力和状态信息。

Method: 提出Kepler-Encoder-v0.1，将机器人状态（关节角、力/扭矩）作为独立模态，与视觉、本体感知通过可学习的交叉注意力层融合到共享潜空间。采用LeJEPA/SIGReg目标进行掩码跨模态预测自监督训练。推理时仅输入视觉，但训练时的多模态融合迫使潜变量编码了相机弱项信息。

Result: 在RH20T数据集上，视觉潜变量对末端执行器状态和力的恢复显著优于原始冻结ViT特征和计算量匹配的纯视觉基线；对电机状态（相机可见）与最强视觉基线持平；跨四个机器人泛化；潜变量的跨模态预测误差可作为无训练异常检测器（AUROC 0.90/0.69）；扩散解码器可从潜变量重建相机帧。

Conclusion: 单时间步验证有效：训练时融合状态信息能使纯视觉推理时的潜变量携带相机弱项信息（力/接触），且跨实施例泛化。下一步是原生帧率的时间融合。

Abstract: A robot must understand the state of its own body, but a camera sees only part of it. Force and contact leave almost no trace in a single frame, and raw vision features read force at $R^2$ at or below $0.10$ on every robot we test. We present Kepler-Encoder-v0.1, a robot-first multimodal encoder that treats robot state as a modality and fuses vision, proprioception, and force/torque into a single shared latent with a learned-query cross-attention layer, trained self-supervised by masked cross-modal prediction under the LeJEPA/SIGReg objective. At evaluation only vision enters, which poses a sharp question. Does fusing state into training make the vision-only latent carry anything the pixels do not already contain? On the RH20T corpus the answer is yes, precisely where the camera is weakest. On held-out scenes, the vision-only latent recovers end-effector state, and force in particular, significantly above both raw frozen-ViT features and a compute-matched vision-only control on every sensored robot, though absolute force recovery at a single timestep is modest; on motor state, which the camera largely sees, it is statistically tied with the strongest vision baselines, and it is the only feature whose latent geometry tracks state. A single embodiment-agnostic encoder covers four robots, and a data-matched control shows this breadth reflects embodiment diversity rather than data volume. The frozen latent is directly useful. Its own cross-modal prediction error is a training-free invalid-state monitor (AUROC $0.90$ on out-of-range states, $0.69$ on scene-swapped states), and a diffusion decoder (PixNerd) reconstructs the camera frame from the latent, confirming the spatial compression preserves world-state. This report validates the single-timestep case; native-rate temporal fusion is the next step.

</details>


### [115] [COLMAR: Cooperative View Policy Learning for Multi-Agent Active 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13524)
*Phu Pham,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COLMAR提出了一种多智能体协同视点策略学习框架，通过共享策略优化和重建感知目标函数，实现多机器人主动3D重建中的高效协作，在GLEAM和Replica数据集上相比基线提升最高54%的精度和49%的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体主动3D重建中存在观测冗余和空间聚集等协调低效问题，严重制约了有限感知预算下的重建质量。

Method: 将视点分配建模为基于地图中心观测的共享策略优化问题，提出重建感知目标函数（重叠感知覆盖、团队级探索、防碰撞），使用参数共享PPO训练策略，部署时每个智能体独立选择动作（无智能体间通信），采用3DGS进行高保真光度评估。

Result: 在GLEAM和Replica数据集上，相比启发式和非协作基线，重建精度提升54%、覆盖率提升49%。

Conclusion: 基于地图中心的共享策略学习可以有效解决多智能体主动重建中的协调问题，无需智能体间通信即可实现高效协作。

Abstract: Active 3D reconstruction requires selecting informative viewpoints under limited sensing budgets. In multi-agent settings, coordination inefficiencies such as redundant observations and spatial clustering can significantly reduce reconstruction quality. We present COLMAR, a cooperative view policy learning framework for multi-agent active 3D reconstruction. COLMAR formulates viewpoint allocation as a shared policy optimization over map-centric observations and introduces a reconstruction-aware objective that promotes overlap-aware coverage, team-level discovery, and collision-safe exploration. Dense feedback derived from incremental reconstruction updates aligns exploration behavior with downstream geometric quality. The policy is trained using parameter-sharing Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with independent per-agent action selection at deployment, conditioned on a fused team map and without inter-agent message passing for decision making. Selected viewpoints are then reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for high-fidelity photometric evaluation. Experiments on GLEAM and Replica demonstrate consistent improvements over heuristic and non-cooperative baselines, achieving up to 54% higher reconstruction accuracy and 49% greater coverage under matched sensing budgets.

</details>


### [116] [Flow-aware Optimal Navigation in Unsteady Flows through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13553)
*Andrea Maria Braghin,Nicolò Botteghi,Matteo Tomasetto,Andrea Manzoni,Gabriele Cazzulani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用TD3强化学习算法训练智能体在混沌双陀螺流中导航，评估五种类脑观测策略（位置、速度、涡量及短期记忆变体），发现速度感知+记忆策略性能最优，涡量传感器提供更优结构映射但牺牲能效，显式全局流参数反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时变流体环境中的自主导航因部分可观测性和不可预测性极具挑战，经典控制需要全局流场先验知识不切实际，而生物系统能利用局部感知线索成功导航，因此希望探索类脑感知机制在强化学习导航中的有效性。

Method: 采用TD3深度强化学习算法，在参数化混沌双陀螺流中训练智能体，设计五种类脑观测策略（相对位置、局部速度、局部涡量及短期记忆变体），同时对比是否提供显式全局流参数的影响。

Result: 感知速度并具备记忆的智能体性能最高；速度感知智能体能效最优，涡量感知智能体结构映射更好、目标接近度更优；显式全局流参数反而降低导航性能。

Conclusion: 强化学习自主系统在隐式流场表征下能学到更鲁棒和通用的策略，显式全局参数有害；结果对类脑机器人导航从仿真迁移到真实环境有指导意义。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic navigation in nonstationary time-varying fluid flows remains a fundamental challenge due to partial observability and the unpredictability of realistic environments. While classical optimal control frameworks employed in robotics require unrealistic a-priori global flow knowledge, biological systems are able to navigate successfully by exploiting localized sensory cues. In this work we present a reinforcement learning approach using the TD3 algorithm to train autonomous agents to reach arbitrary targets within a parametric, chaotic double-gyre flow. To investigate optimal sensory mechanisms, we evaluate five bio-inspired observation strategies based on relative position, local velocity or local vorticity measures, and short-term memory variants. Additionally, we analyze the impact of providing agents with explicit global flow parameters. Numerical results demonstrate that an agent that is able to sense and remember a set number of flow velocity measures achieves the highest performance. The experiments reveal a trade-off in sensor utility: velocity-aware agents optimize energy efficiency, whereas vorticity sensors provide superior structural mapping and achieve better target proximity. Incorporating explicit global flow parameters is shown to decrease navigation performance. This behavior suggests that reinforcement learning-based autonomous systems develop more robust and general policies when restricted to implicit flow representations. The presented results offer insights for improving the transition of bio-inspired robotic navigation from simulation to real-world environments.

</details>


### [117] [IMMNet: Hybrid Fusion of Model-based and Data-driven Approaches for Maneuvering Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13573)
*Yixuan Zhao,Chaoqun Yang,Lin Gao,Yongxiao Tian,Ting Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMMNet混合模型/数据驱动算法，将交互多模型(IMM)的可解释结构与可学习神经组件结合，用于三维空间机动目标跟踪，优于现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维空间机动目标跟踪因复杂运动动力学和模型失配而具有挑战性，现有纯模型驱动或纯数据驱动方法各有局限。

Method: 提出的IMMNet算法保留IMM的贝叶斯推理机制（保证可解释性和实时性），同时引入可学习神经组件自适应学习运动模式和噪声特性。

Result: 在多场景实验中，IMMNet持续优于现有算法，表现出鲁棒性、可解释性和实用性。

Conclusion: IMMNet是鲁棒、可解释且实用的机动目标跟踪解决方案，兼具模型驱动的可解释性和数据驱动的适应性。

Abstract: Maneuvering target tracking in three-dimensional space remains a challenging problem due to complex motion dynamics and model mismatch. To address this, this paper proposes a hybrid model/data-driven algorithm named IMMNet, which integrates the interpretable structure of the interacting multiple model (IMM) algorithm with learnable neural components. Unlike end-to-end black-box methods, the proposed IMMNet algorithm not only can preserve the Bayesian inference mechanism that is essential for real-time radar applications, but also can adaptively learn motion patterns and noise characteristics from data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed IMMNet algorithm consistently outperforms the existing algorithms across various scenarios, validating it as a robust, interpretable, and practical solution for maneuvering target tracking.

</details>


### [118] [Agile perceptive multi-skill locomotion for quadrupedal robots in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13579)
*Jun-Gill Kang,Jaehyun Park,Tae-Gyu Song,Joon-Ha Kim,Seungwoo Hong,Hae-Won Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APT-RL是一个统一框架，通过轨迹优化生成大规模2D运动数据集，训练多样化可复用的四足机器人运动技能，并利用这些技能作为先验高效学习下游任务，最终在真实机器人上实现基于机载感知的多技能自主切换高速穿越复杂地形（室内障碍和野外环境），峰值速度达6米/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在复杂地形（崎岖户外到城市景观）中需要无缝集成多种运动技能、平滑步态切换以及仅用机载传感器的高速感知运动，现有方法缺乏统一的能够实现自主技能切换和多技能高速穿越的框架。

Method: 1) 通过带简化动力学的轨迹优化生成大规模、特征丰富的2D运动数据集；2) 基于这些数据集训练多样化、可复用的 locomotion skills；3) 将这些高质量技能作为强先验，通过强化学习高效学习复杂下游任务；4) 将2D技能自然扩展到3D环境，部署为统一策略实现多技能自主切换。

Result: 在真实四足机器人上验证：能够穿越室内复杂障碍和野外环境，完成动态下落等敏捷动作，瞬时峰值速度达6米/秒；单一机载策略可稳健穿越楼梯、障碍物、踏脚石、间隙、倒下的树枝等多种障碍物。

Conclusion: APT-RL证明了利用简化的2D轨迹优化生成运动技能先验，再通过强化学习高效学习复杂下游任务，能够在真实四足机器人上实现仅依赖机载感知的多技能自主切换高速穿越复杂地形，提供了一种可扩展的统一框架。

Abstract: Enabling quadrupedal robots to traverse complex terrains-from rugged outdoor environments to urban landscapes-requires seamless integration of multiple motor skills, smooth transitions between gaits, and high-speed perceptive locomotion using only onboard sensors. We present APT-RL (Action Pretrained Transformer-based Reinforcement Learning), a unified framework that enables multi-skill locomotion to achieve high-speed traversal in complex environments through autonomous skill transitions utilizing only onboard perception and computation. Our approach generates large-scale, feature-rich 2D motion datasets through trajectory optimization with simplified dynamics. These datasets enable training of diverse, reusable locomotion skills that transfer effectively to a real quadruped robot operating on complex uneven terrains. The resulting high-quality skills serve as strong priors for efficient learning of complex downstream tasks and extend naturally to 3D environments, enabling smooth, high-speed multi-skill locomotion in deployed policy. Real-world experiments demonstrate the framework's capabilities: the robot performs agile maneuvers through complex indoor obstacles and outdoor wild environments, including dynamic drop-down maneuvers that reach instantaneous peak speeds of up to 6 meters per second. A single onboard policy enables robust traversal of diverse obstacles, including stairs, hurdles, stepping stones, gaps, and fallen branches, demonstrating the versatility and effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [119] [Active Trust Management for Successful Human-Robot Teaming: Moving from a Trust Repair to a Trust Satisficing Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13595)
*Nicola Webb,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 讨论人类-机器人团队中的信任管理问题，提出信任满意化视角和包含在线信任度量、闭环自适应行为、可变自主性的信任管理框架，强调信任对高风险动态环境下人机团队成功的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人可能犯错或与团队目标冲突，导致信任下降并影响任务成功，因此需要主动信任管理来保障人机团队在动态高风险环境中的有效性。

Method: 提出信任满意化视角，构建信任管理框架：在线测量信任代理指标、闭环自适应调整机器人行为、可变自主性为人类保留价值判断空间。参考了"快速信任"实验及行为信任度量方法。

Result: 提出了一个概念性信任管理框架，初步实验探索了"快速信任"和行为信任度量，尚未给出系统性的实验验证或量化结果。

Conclusion: 主动信任管理是人机团队成功的重要前提，信任是动态、多面、依赖上下文的，需要闭环管理机制；同时指出了后续需要进一步研究的问题。

Abstract: Integrating mobile robots into human teams promises significant capability improvements for tasks such as searching hazardous environments. Unlike existing teleoperated robots, future robot systems will increasingly be endowed with some level of artificial intelligence (AI), giving them a degree of autonomy in how they pursue mission goals. This autonomy could make a human-agent (robot) team more effective but also put inter-agent trust under strain if robots make a mistake, or (appear to) pursue task priorities that conflict with the team's best interest. During a mission, agents' trust states are anticipated to vary according to the situation as understood by each teammate (trustor). If component-level (agent) or system-level trust falls below sufficient levels for cooperative tasks to be completed, it could critically affect mission success . We argue that active trust management will be an important precondition for the success of human-robot teams (HRTs, a subcategory of human-agent teams with embodied agents), especially in dynamic, high-risk environments. We present a trust satisficing perspective which acknowledges and attempts to account for the fluctuating, multi-faceted, and context-dependent nature of trust and trust requirements even under normal operating conditions. Our outline of a trust management framework for human-robot teaming includes online measurement of proxy metrics for trust, closed-loop adaptation of robot behavior, and variable autonomy to give space for human responsibility in situations requiring value judgements. We refer to a recent experimental exploration of 'swift trust' and a novel behavioral trust metric for HRT, and we highlight issues for further investigation.

</details>


### [120] [Semantic Anchoring for Robotic Action Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13597)
*Yuan Xu,Youheng Shi,Chengyang Li,Wentao Zhu,Yizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型在微调过程中丢失了预训练语义结构，本文通过锚定动作表示到语义流形并分解为共享语义通道和私有通道，在推理时丢弃私有通道，在仿真和真实世界基准上实现最高+18.7%分布内和+21.5%分布外泛化提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型从预训练视觉语言模型继承丰富语义，但在有限机器人数据微调后会退化该结构并损害泛化。核心问题是：什么是好的动作表示？受镜像神经元理论启发，检查机器人动作表示是否保持预训练编码器捕获的语义结构。

Method: 系统性探针实验验证微调中语义结构退化及其与任务成功和分布外泛化的同步关系；提出即插即用方法，将动作表示锚定到语义流形，同时分解为共享语义通道和私有通道，推理时丢弃私有通道，模型部署不变。

Result: 在不同VLA骨干网络、仿真和真实世界基准上，分布内任务提升最高+18.7%，分布外泛化提升最高+21.5%。

Conclusion: 动作表示应保持语义结构，所提方法有效改善VLA微调中的语义退化问题，提升泛化能力，且不改变推理时模型结构。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit rich semantic representations from pretrained Vision-Language Models, yet fine-tuning on limited robot demonstrations degrades this structure and undermines generalization. A fundamental question therefore arises: what constitutes a good action representation? Inspired by the mirror neuron theory's insight that observation and execution share an intention-level encoding, we examine whether a robot's action representations preserve the semantic structure captured by pretrained encoders. Systematic probing confirms that this structure erodes during finetuning, and that its quality synchronizes with both task success and out-of-distribution generalization. We further introduce a plug-and-play method that anchors action representations to a semantic manifold while decomposing representations into a shared semantic channel and a private channel, all discarded at inference, leaving the deployed model unchanged. Validated on different VLA backbones across simulation and real-world benchmarks, our method yields up to +18.7% on real-world in-distribution tasks and +21.5% on out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [121] [An Empirical Study on Stage-Information Interfaces for VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13605)
*Yingwei Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 探索在长程操作任务中，使用分段动作标注作为中间表示，通过进度模块追踪当前阶段，并将阶段信息以文本或归一化序号形式输入给VLA策略。实验表明，显式阶段信息并不自动提升策略性能，但在继续微调设置下，序号阶段状态（Ordinal Stage-State）在全部三次配对运行中均优于完整任务指令和当前阶段文本两种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作任务中一条高级指令涵盖多个动作阶段，现有VLA模型直接端到端学习完整指令到动作的映射，缺乏对任务阶段结构的显式建模，导致长程任务成功率受限。

Method: 使用分段动作标注作为中间表示，设计进度模块追踪当前激活阶段；将阶段信息以当前阶段文本或归一化序号阶段状态两种形式输入动作策略；在GR00T N1.6上基于LIBERO-10进行直接微调和从完整任务指令基线继续微调两种设置对比。

Result: 直接微调下，完整任务指令、当前阶段文本和序号阶段状态的平均成功率分别为57.45%、50.24%和54.36%。继续微调下对应均值分别为49.07%、50.00%和53.75%，其中序号阶段状态在全部三次配对运行中均超越两种替代方案。

Conclusion: 显式阶段信息不自动提升策略性能，效果因接口表示形式和训练安排而异；序号阶段状态在继续微调设置下具有一致优势。

Abstract: One high-level instruction in long-horizon manipulation can cover several action stages. We use segmented action annotations as an intermediate representation between the full-task instruction and VLA action chunks. A progress module tracks the active stage, while the action policy receives stage information either as current-stage text or as a normalized ordinal stage index in robot state. We compare these interfaces with GR00T N1.6 on LIBERO-10 under direct fine-tuning and continuation fine-tuning from a full-task instruction baseline. Under direct fine-tuning, full-task instruction, current-stage text, and Ordinal Stage-State achieve mean success rates of 57.45%, 50.24%, and 54.36%, respectively, showing that explicit stage information does not automatically improve the policy. Under continuation, the corresponding means are 49.07%, 50.00%, and 53.75%, with Ordinal Stage-State exceeding both alternatives in all three paired runs. The observed benefit differs across interface representations and training arrangements.

</details>


### [122] [Design, Modeling and Experimental Validation of a Miniature Hybrid Underwater Glider With Large-Range Foldable Deflectable Wings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13622)
*Yongjian Zhu,Yusen Tao,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FoDeGlider，一种配备可大幅折叠偏转机翼的微型混合水下滑翔机，建立了基于多体动力学和CRBA投影的统一Fossen模型，提出顺序参数辨识框架并开源基准数据集，通过实验验证了变构型动力学建模与主动形态重构能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型混合水下滑翔机在长期海洋观测和受限空间巡检中需求增长，大范围机翼重构可同时增强机动性和形状适应性，但引入机械集成、动力学建模和水动力学表征方面的重大挑战，急需系统性的解决方案。

Method: 1) 设计双独立驱动可折叠偏转机翼的微型滑翔机FoDeGlider；2) 将机翼构型作为结构变量，建立多体动力学模型；3) 基于CRBA投影将复合惯量、力螺旋变换和部件级水动力学统一为Fossen形式动力学模型；4) 提出顺序参数辨识框架，估计机身和机翼水动力系数并开源基准数据集。

Result: 实验表明所提动力学模型和参数辨识在不同构型下均准确；门框穿越实验验证了FoDeGlider在运动过程中主动重构形态的能力，实现了受限水下环境中的增强导航。

Conclusion: 大范围机翼重构可有效提升微型水下滑翔机的机动性和形状适应性，所提多体动力学建模与参数辨识方法为此类系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Miniature hybrid underwater gliders have attracted increasing attention for long-endurance ocean observation and confined-space inspection. Large-range wing reconfiguration offers a promising yet largely unexplored approach for simultaneously enhancing maneuverability and shape adaptability in constrained underwater environments. However, such morphing introduces substantial challenges in mechanical integration, dynamic modeling, and hydrodynamic characterization. This paper presents FoDeGlider, a miniature hybrid underwater glider equipped with two independently actuated wings capable of large-range folding and deflection. To capture configuration-dependent variations in mass distribution, center-of-geometry location, and hydrodynamic loading, a multibody dynamics model is developed by treating wing configuration as a structural variable. A composite rigid body algorithm (CRBA)-based projection formulates the composite inertia, wrench transformations, and component-level hydrodynamics into a unified Fossen-form dynamic model applicable to arbitrary wing configurations. A sequential parameter-identification framework is further proposed to estimate fuselage and wing hydrodynamic coefficients, resulting in an open benchmark dataset for model identification and validation. Extensive experiments are conducted, the results of which demonstrate accurate dynamic modeling and parameter identification across diverse morphing configurations. Gate traversal experiments further validate FoDeGlider's ability to actively reconfigure its morphology during locomotion, enabling enhanced navigation in confined underwater environments.

</details>


### [123] [From Language to Navigation Goals: A Vision-Language Approach for Semantic Navigation of Mobile Robots Using RGB-D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13624)
*Jose Martínez-Fajardo,Pablo Pueyo,Fernando Caballero,Luis Merino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于ROS 2的模块化语言驱动导航框架，通过自然语言指令解析目标物体、RGB-D定位并生成导航目标，在TurtleBot3和Unitree Go2上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专家用户难以通过传统方式与机器人交互，需要一种直观的自然语言接口来驱动移动机器人执行导航任务，将语言理解、环境感知和自主导航无缝集成。

Method: 构建模块化ROS 2组件，接收自然语言请求→识别目标物体→利用RGB-D数据估计目标位置→生成导航目标→通过Nav2堆栈执行导航，同时生成自然语言反馈。

Result: 在TurtleBot3 Waffle和Unitree Go2（配RealSense相机）的仿真和真实场景中，系统能正确解释直接指令和上下文请求，导航至目标并生成有意义的反馈。

Conclusion: 证明了将语义感知与自主导航结合实现直观人机交互范式的可行性，代码将开源。

Abstract: Natural language interaction provides an intuitive way for non-expert users to communicate with robotic platforms. However, transforming user requests into executable navigation actions remains a challenging task, requiring the integration of language understanding, environment perception, and autonomous navigation. This work presents a language-driven navigation framework that enables mobile robots to interpret user requests in natural language to move the robot to a destination and autonomously navigate towards it.
  The framework is composed of modular ROS 2 components that cooperate to transform natural language instructions into navigation actions. Given a natural language request referring to a target in the environment (e.g., "go to the mail box"), the system identifies the referenced object, estimates its position using RGB-D data, and generates a navigation goal, which is then executed through the ROS 2 Nav2 navigation stack. The ROS 2-based implementation facilitates portability across different robotic platforms, requiring only the configuration of the corresponding topics and services.
  The system is evaluated in both simulation and real-world scenarios using a TurtleBot3 Waffle and a Unitree Go2 robot with a RealSense camera. Experimental results show that the framework successfully interprets both direct commands and contextual requests, generates meaningful natural-language feedback, and navigates towards the desired target. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining semantic perception and autonomous navigation to provide an intuitive human-robot interaction paradigm. Code will be released as open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [124] [Design and Control of the "QuadBoat": A Quadruped Surface Vehicle for Drowning Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13633)
*Lianxin Zhang,Yihan Huang,Huihuan Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QuadBoat，一种仿生四足无人水面艇（USV），通过可主动调节姿态实现高适应性敏捷机动，采用逆运动学控制器和级联MPC-PID控制器实现对水面目标的精确跟踪与打捞，室内外实验验证了其机动性、跟踪精度和救援有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水面救援中快速打捞受害者至关重要，但现有救援机器人研究很少直接解决这一问题，亟需兼具高机动性和精确目标跟踪能力的无人水面艇。

Method: 设计四足机器人构型USV（QuadBoat），通过主动姿态调节实现高适应性机动；采用逆运动学控制器和级联模型预测控制（MPC）-PID控制器实现整体运动控制；视觉引导的目标跟踪与物体拾取。

Result: 机动性演示验证了QuadBoat的高敏捷性；腿部动作跟踪和轨迹跟踪实验确认了高运动精度和系统机动性；室内外视觉跟踪和物体拾取实验验证了目标跟踪能力和救援有效性。

Conclusion: QuadBoat作为仿生四足USV，成功实现了水面目标的精确跟踪与打捞，为水面救援机器人提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Prompt extraction of victims from water is crucial in water surface rescue missions. However, previous research on rescue robots has seldom addressed this issue. This paper presents QuadBoat, a bio-inspired unmanned surface vehicle (USV) designed to track and retrieve victims from water. QuadBoat features a quadrupedal robot configuration, enabling it with highly adaptable and agile maneuverability through its actively adjustable posture. Employing an inverse kinematics-based controller and cascaded model predictive control (MPC)-PID controller for overall movement, QuadBoat can accurately track and retrieve objects on the water surface. Maneuverability demonstrations validate QuadBoat's high agility, while a series of tracking experiments, including leg action tracking and trajectory tracking, confirm its high motion accuracy and system mobility. Finally, visual-based tracking and object pickup experiments further verify QuadBoat's target tracking capabilities and its effectiveness in executing rescues, both indoors and outdoors.

</details>


### [125] [Anatomy of Uncertainty: Expressive Descriptors of Robotic Manipulator Motion for Non-verbal Communication in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13696)
*Ridhima Bector,Souravik Dutta,Poornima Ramachandran,Ree Yan Yeoh,Jui Hien Tan,Domenico Campolo,Bernhard Johannes Schmitt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个数学框架，通过机器人操纵器运动表达感知不确定性。基于Laban运动分析，将行为映射到置信度-警觉性状态空间，用五个运动原语和11个运动学/几何描述符参数化。通过视频人类实验验证了四种轨迹的可识别性和描述符对感知强度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中机器人需要传达因不完整或模糊感知而产生的**不确定性**，而现有机器人运动表达缺乏对感知不确定性的系统化数学框架。

Method: 基于Laban运动分析构建Commitment-Vigilance状态空间，将不确定性状态（置信、好奇、犹豫、恐惧、静止）映射到Effort特征；设计五个运动原语并利用11个运动学和几何描述符参数化；通过视频人类受试者实验评估识别效果和描述符影响。

Result: 参与者能可靠识别机器人意图的行为状态，多个描述符显著调节了表达力强度。

Conclusion: 建立了感知上可验证的机器人不确定性运动编码基础，支持通过参数化运动表示为协作任务生成自主轨迹。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-robot collaboration must communicate not only their intended actions but also uncertainty arising from incomplete or ambiguous perception. This work introduces a mathematical framework for expressing perceptual uncertainty through robotic manipulator motion. Drawing on Laban Movement Analysis, robot behavior is organized in a Commitment-Vigilance state space that maps uncertainty-related states - confidence, curiosity, hesitance, fear, and inactivity - to distinct Laban Effort signatures. Five motion primitives - approach, pause, retreat, exploration, and oscillation - are then parameterized using eleven kinematic and geometric descriptors, including acceleration, pause and retreat characteristics, gaze angles, tilt, and shiver amplitude. A video-based human-subject study evaluated recognition of four expressive trajectories and the influence of individual descriptors on perceived intensity. Participants reliably identified the intended behavioral states, while several descriptors significantly modulated expressiveness. The results establish a perceptually grounded basis for encoding robot uncertainty in motion and support future autonomous trajectory generation using parametric movement representations for collaborative tasks in shared environments. Code, videos, questionnaire and appendices are available at "https://bit.ly/github-aou".

</details>


### [126] [Towards a Modular Bin-picking Framework for Handling Object Pose Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13698)
*Frederik Hagelskjær*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个模块化框架，联合处理机器人抓取任务中的物体姿态不确定性和抓取误差，通过姿态分布估计、第二视角融合和独立抓取补偿模块实现鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人精确抓取方法通常只针对特定挑战（姿态估计误差或抓取误差之一），缺乏通用解决方案，且实际场景中姿态不确定性和抓取误差常同时出现。

Method: 模块化框架，包含：(1) 物体姿态分布估计模块处理姿态不确定性；(2) 第二视角模块计算互补姿态分布并融合以降低不确定性；(3) 两个独立模块补偿抓取误差。模块可组合使用或单独使用。

Result: 在真实场景中用三种不同物体进行无误差评估，所有模块均能提升效率。

Conclusion: 联合处理姿态分布和抓取姿态误差是构建更灵活、可靠的机器人生产系统的有前景方向；当前限于SO(2)姿态不确定性，可扩展至SE(3)。

Abstract: In recent years, there has been growing interest in robust robotic systems for precise bin-picking applications. To achieve reliable performance, such systems must address errors arising from both the object pose estimation and the grasping process. Although various approaches have been proposed, they typically target specific challenges and do not offer general solutions. In this paper, we present a modular framework that jointly handles both error types. The framework incorporates object pose distribution estimation to account for pose uncertainty, which frequently arises in situations with ambiguous observations where a single correct pose cannot be determined. To further reduce uncertainty, we introduce a second-viewpoint module that computes complementary pose distributions, which are subsequently fused. This fusion decreases overall uncertainty and improves system efficiency. Additionally, two independent modules are included to compensate for grasping errors. The modular design allows the components to be combined for optimal performance or used individually, depending on the physical setup.
  The proposed method is evaluated in a real-world setup with three different objects, with no errors, and all modules are shown to improve efficiency. These results suggest that incorporating pose distributions with grasping pose errors is a promising direction for developing more flexible and reliable robotic production systems.
  To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework that jointly addresses both grasping and object pose uncertainties using interchangeable modules. We believe there is ample opportunity to integrate additional modules, resulting in improved performance and flexibility. The current framework is limited to pose uncertainties in SO(2), but it could be extended to SE(3), enabling additional modules to improve the system.

</details>


### [127] [nuTruck: Benchmarking Autonomous Driving Planning for Distributed Electric-drive Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13704)
*Jinyu Miao,Pu Zhang,Yifei He,Chengyao Zhang,Kun Jiang,Ke Wang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向分布式电驱动重卡（DET）的自动驾驶规划器高保真基准nuTruck，集成非线性动力学模型以评估侧翻风险，并在nuPlan真实场景上对多种规划器进行闭环评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型规划器在乘用车上成功，但在动力学复杂、侧翻风险高的分布式电驱动重卡（DET）上缺乏专门的高保真评估基准，阻碍了相关研究与应用。

Method: 1) 将高精度非线性卡车动力学模型集成到仿真中，支持全轮独立驱动/转向并捕捉加速/减速/转弯时的动态载荷转移；2) 适配多种基于规则和学习的规划器作为基线；3) 基于nuPlan真实驾驶场景进行大规模闭环评估。

Result: 在nuPlan真实场景上完成了闭环评测，不仅分析了常规无碰撞规划性能，还定量评估了规划轨迹的动力学安全性（侧翻风险）。

Conclusion: nuTruck可作为DET自动驾驶规划器公平、真实评估的新标准基准。

Abstract: The dominance of traditional rule-based methods in autonomous driving has gradually been replaced by learning-based approaches. While learning-based planners have achieved considerable success in passenger vehicles, their performance on heavy-duty trucks, particularly modern distributed electric-drive trucks (DETs), remains largely unexplored. To facilitate research and application of learning-based planners in DETs, this letter presents the first high-fidelity benchmark, called nuTruck, designed to support large-scale neural network training and closed-loop evaluation. Given the complex dynamics and high rollover susceptibility of DETs, we first incorporate a highly accurate nonlinear truck dynamical model into the simulation, which enables independent driving and steering of all wheels and captures dynamic load transfer caused by acceleration, deceleration, and cornering, thereby allowing quantitative assessment of rollover risk in closed-loop simulation. Second, we adapt several rule-based and learning-based planners as baselines for DETs and evaluate their performance in closed-loop simulation. Finally, using real-world driving scenarios from the nuPlan dataset, we conduct extensive closed-loop evaluations, analyzing not only conventional collision-free planning performance, but also the dynamical safety of the planned trajectories. The proposed nuTruck benchmark is expected to serve as a new standard for fair and realistic evaluation of autonomous driving planners on DETs.

</details>


### [128] [The Nonsmooth Impact Direction (NSID) of Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13768)
*Annika Kirner,Christian Ott*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统分析了刚性连杆机器人与刚性环境碰撞中的非光滑冲击方向（NSID），证明其是冲击特征方向且几乎独立于接触属性，为冲击规划与控制提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献将刚性机器人碰撞建模为瞬时事件，但缺乏对碰撞前后速度方向的系统分析，特别是冲击力方向与接触属性的关系尚不明确，阻碍了冲击相关控制算法的设计。

Method: 从非光滑动力学理论出发，形式化定义非光滑冲击方向（NSID），分析其在无弹性/柔性关节、无约束/约束系统中的特性，并通过实验验证理论预测。

Result: NSID是机器人冲击的特征方向，几乎独立于接触属性；接近方向相对于NSID决定了摩擦非弹性碰撞中冲击力的方向；理论分析得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 该理论分析可作为未来机器人冲击应用（如湿滑表面人形行走、重复锤击）中规划与控制算法的基础。

Abstract: Collisions of rigid-link robots and rigid environments are often modeled as instantaneous events. Under this idealization, the impact forces become impulsive and the system velocities nonsmooth. In this work, we systematically analyze pre- and post-impact velocities focusing on what we refer to as the nonsmooth impact direction (NSID). We show that it is a characteristic direction of a robotic impact and largely independent of contact properties. The results are directly applicable to large classes of backdrivable robotic systems with rigid links. We address particularities of systems with nonelastic and flexible joints, unconstrained as well as constrained systems. Further, we show that the approach direction w.r.t the NSID sets the direction of the impulsive force in frictional, inelastic impacts. The comprehensive theoretical analysis of this work supported by an experimental validation may serve as a foundation for future planning and control algorithms for various robotic impact applications. These can include humanoid locomotion on a slippery surface or repetitive hammering.

</details>


### [129] [Dynamical Vehicle Orienteering Problem for Multi-Rotor Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13789)
*František Nekovář,Matej Novosad,Martin Saska,Robert Pěnička*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动力车辆路径问题(DVOP)，将车辆动力学（三维质点模型，考虑重力、速度/加速度约束）引入定向越野问题，实现奖励最大化与时间最优轨迹规划的耦合。提出BnB（NLP+MILP）精确求解和LNS元启发式方法，在基准测试上比KOP最优结果提升最高37%，并在多旋翼无人机上实际部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定向越野问题(OP)忽略车辆动力学，无法直接用于真实无人机的飞行规划。本文旨在将完整的动力学约束（重力、速度/加速度限幅）与奖励最大化统一建模，弥合轨迹优化与组合优化之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 形式化DVOP，用三维质点模型(PMM)描述多旋翼无人机动力学，飞行时间为预算；2) BnB方法：基于最小时间轨迹原语（通过三目标点）构造新型MILP给出紧的奖励上界，结合NLP求解子问题；3) LNS元启发式：用有限推力分解快速计算高质量PMM轨迹，提供初始奖励界并扩展到大算例。

Result: 在基准实例上，相比Kinematic Orienteering Problem最优解提升高达37%；在多旋翼无人机上真实世界部署验证了PMM轨迹的可行性。

Conclusion: DVOP框架有效统一了任务规划与动力学约束，提出的BnB和LNS方法能在保持严格动力学模型的同时获得高质量解，真实部署验证了实用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Dynamical Vehicle Orienteering Problem (DVOP), a generalization of the Orienteering Problem (OP). The OP maximizes the reward collected from spatial targets under a limited travel budget; the DVOP extends it by accounting for both external and vehicle-actuated forces. We study the DVOP in the context of multi-rotor Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) flight planning, using a three-dimensional Point-Mass Model (PMM) constrained by maximum velocity and acceleration magnitudes and subject to gravitational acceleration, with the travel budget expressed as a maximum flight time. Because the DVOP couples reward maximization with time-optimal trajectory planning, it cannot be formulated as a simple graph problem and solved exactly without relaxing or under-actuating the vehicle dynamics. We therefore propose two solution approaches: a Branch-and-Bound (BnB) procedure that combines Non-Linear Programming (NLP) and Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) to provide high-quality solutions, and a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) metaheuristic that supplies an initial reward bound and scales to instances intractable for the BnB. The BnB relies on a novel MILP formulation of travel costs based on minimum-time trajectory primitives through target triplets, yielding a tight reward upper bound, while the LNS uses limited thrust decomposition to compute fast, high-quality PMM trajectories. Experiments on benchmark instances show improvements of up to 37 % over state-of-the-art solutions for the Kinematic Orienteering Problem, and a real-world deployment on a multi-rotor UAV verifies the proposed PMM solution trajectories.

</details>


### [130] [Vision-Based Obstacle Separation for Strawberry Harvesting in Clusters Using Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13799)
*Teng Li,Hanfei Shi,Chunjiang Zhao,Ya Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出层次化强化学习框架VGPA，结合视觉引导决策机制和渐进式自适应探索策略(PAES)，用于草莓簇状环境中遮挡果实的分离与采摘。模拟成功率96.7%，sim-to-real成功率达71.7%-88.3%，仅比直接采摘多耗时1.22秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 簇状草莓环境中成熟果实常被未成熟果实遮挡，直接抓取不可靠，亟需一种能先分离遮挡物再抓取目标果实的智能采摘方法。

Method: 将任务分解为遮挡物分离和目标抓取两个顺序阶段；高层用视觉引导机制优化选项选择并加速策略收敛；低层用PAES提升连续控制学习中的探索效率和训练稳定性。

Result: 模拟成功率96.7%；真机sim-to-real成功率71.7%-88.3%，平均采摘时间仅比直接采摘多1.22秒。

Conclusion: 验证了该方法在复杂簇状环境下机器人采摘的有效性、泛化能力和实用潜力。

Abstract: Selective harvesting in clustered strawberry environments is challenging because ripe fruits are often occluded by surrounding unripe fruits, making direct grasping unreliable. To address this problem, this paper proposes a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework, termed VGPA, which integrates a vision-guided decision mechanism and a Progressive Adaptive Exploration Strategy (PAES) for vision-based obstacle separation and harvesting. The task was decomposed into two sequential stages: obstacle separation and target grasping. At the high level, the vision-guided mechanism improved option selection and accelerated policy convergence. At the low level, PAES improved exploration efficiency and training stability during continuous control learning. In simulation experiments, the learned policy achieved a success rate of 96.7%. In addition, sim-to-real transfer experiments on a self-developed parallel robot showed that the proposed method achieved success rates ranging from 71.7% to 88.3%, outperforming direct picking while requiring only 1.22~s more average harvesting time. These results verified the effectiveness, generalization ability, and practical potential of the proposed method for robotic harvesting in complex clustered environments.

</details>


### [131] [A Deployed Hybrid Vehicle-in-the-Loop Platform for Validating Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13806)
*Anastasia Bolovinou,Giorgos Hadjipavlis,Markos Antonopoulos,Panagiotis Tachtalis,Konstantinos Petousakis,Konstantinos Lazaridis,Alexandros Siskos,Bill Roungas,Angelos Amditis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了一个混合式车辆在环（ViL）平台，将真实车辆与CARLA数字孪生通过V2X消息管道连接，利用GPU加速的协同感知模块在场景运行时融合概率占用网格，并在多车双T路口场景下验证了其扩展FoV和提升占用单元召回率的能力，同时分析了定位噪声与天气对感知性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲安全法规允许自动驾驶认证证据通过虚拟方式生成，但前提是需要经过验证的物理-虚拟设施。现有平台缺乏将真实车辆与高保真数字孪生实时耦合、并在公共道路代表性场景中运行协同感知的集成方案。

Method: 构建ViL平台：真实车辆通过ETSI合规CAM/CPM消息流将数据传入CARLA数字孪生，GPU加速协同感知模块在场景运行时融合消息生成概率占用网格。在多车双T路口场景下测试，评估正常、雨天、夜间及五种定位噪声水平下的协同感知负载。

Result: 协同感知显著扩展了FoV覆盖并提升占用单元召回率；超过中等定位噪声阈值后，定位不确定性成为主导误差源，而非天气条件。

Conclusion: 该平台已成功实现首次集成运行，并规划向地中海ODD测试服务演进，同时指出了当前架构限制及工程改进目标。

Abstract: European safety regulation now permits a large share of automated-driving homologation evidence to be produced virtually, provided a validated physical-virtual facility generates it. We present a deployed hybrid Vehicle-in-the-Loop (ViL) platform that couples a real instrumented vehicle with a CARLA-based digital twin (DT) through a V2X message pipeline, and we report its first integrated operation on a public-road-representative test track. A real vehicle streams ETSI-compliant CAM/CPM messages into the DT, where a GPU-accelerated Cooperative Perception (CP) module fuses them into a probabilistic occupancy grid during scenario runtime. We demonstrate the platform on a multi-vehicle double T-intersection scenario, characterise the CP workload across nominal, rain and night conditions and five localization-noise levels, and discuss the platform's current architectural limits and the engineering targets they define. The results show that CP substantially widens field-of-view (FoV) coverage and improves occupied-cell recall, and that beyond a moderate localization-noise threshold, positioning uncertainty, and not weather, becomes the dominant error source. We outline the platform's trajectory toward a Mediterranean operational design domain (ODD) testing service.

</details>


### [132] [Learning Robust Execution in Robotic Manipulation with Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13818)
*Xiaopeng Zhang,Yueyang Weng,Qi Liu,Yongjin Mu,Yanjie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两个互补的执行质量度量指标和一个智能体强化学习框架，通过高层决策而非底层动作学习来评估和恢复机器人操作中的执行稳定性，在LIBERO benchmark上取得最高39.2%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏显式的执行稳定性评估机制和异常恢复能力，导致操作过程中误差累积和任务失败。

Method: (1) 两个互补的运行时执行质量度量指标；(2) 智能体强化学习框架，通过推理历史执行轨迹并选择少量执行模式来调节执行过程，触发恢复机制将机器人回退到名义状态。

Result: LIBERO benchmark上标准设置提升13.7%成功率，扰动设置提升39.2%成功率。

Conclusion: 通过高层决策而非底层动作学习能够有效增强VLA模型的执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation poses fundamental challenges due to uncertainty, long-horizon execution, and compounding errors, which can easily destabilize execution and lead to task failure. Although recent vision-language-action (VLA) models exhibit strong generalization, they typically lack explicit mechanisms to assess execution stability and to recover when execution deviates from its nominal behavior. In this paper, we propose: (1) two complementary metrics to assess execution quality at runtime, and (2) an agentic reinforcement learning framework that learns to restore effective execution through high-level decision-making rather than directly learning low-level actions. In this framework, an agentic policy reasons over recent execution history and selects among a small set of execution modes to regulate the execution process. Under execution degradation, it triggers appropriate recovery mechanisms to restore the robot to previously visited nominal states, enabling the task to continue. We evaluate the proposed method on the LIBERO benchmark, achieving up to a 13.7% improvement in success rate under standard settings and up to a 39.2% improvement under disturbance settings, demonstrating substantially enhanced execution robustness.

</details>


### [133] [Recursive ArUco Markers: A Scalable Fiducial Marker Design for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Landing Pads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13830)
*Rafael Munoz-Salinas,Francisco Jose Romero-Ramirez,Sergio Garrido-Jurado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种递归ArUco标记设计，通过改进的比特采样策略在父标记的黑白比特内嵌入完整子标记，实现任意递归深度，支持部分遮挡下的鲁棒检测，并为多无人机编队提供独特着陆点字典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准视觉标记在无人机自主导航中工作范围有限（过远或过近时不可检测），而现有递归/分形标记要么依赖标记中心可见（易受遮挡影响），要么递归深度和放置位置受限，无法满足实际需求。

Method: 采用改进的比特采样策略，在标准ArUco标记的黑白比特内嵌入完整子标记（而非仅中心区域），通过逐层递归检测实现任意深度的多尺度识别，且保持单一唯一ID跨所有递归尺度。

Result: 实现了无限递归深度、部分遮挡下的鲁棒检测、支持多无人机同时操作各自独立着陆点（提供了多个唯一着陆ID字典），克服了Fractal和Harco标记的结构和字典约束限制。

Conclusion: 递归ArUco标记在遮挡鲁棒性、递归深度和可扩展性上优于现有分形/递归标记方法，适用于多无人机协同自主导航和精准着陆场景。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) increasingly rely on visual fiducial markers for autonomous navigation and precision landing. However, standard markers suffer from limited operational ranges, becoming undetectable when the camera is either too far or too close. While recursive and fractal markers have been proposed to address this issue, existing approaches either require the marker's center to remain visible, making them vulnerable to occlusion, or are limited in their recursion depth and placement. We propose a novel Recursive ArUco marker design. Our method allows any standard fiducial marker to be transformed into a recursive marker with an arbitrary depth. By employing a modified bit-sampling strategy during detection, we embed complete markers within both the black and white bits of the parent marker. This approach guarantees unlimited recursion depth and robust detection even with partial occlusion, as it does not rely on the marker's center being visible. Furthermore, by maintaining a single, unique identifier across all recursive scales, our proposal provides an extensive dictionary of multiple unique landing pads. This capability allows fleets of UAVs to operate simultaneously, with each drone landing at its designated location -- a feature not supported by existing Fractal and Harco markers due to their structural and dictionary constraints.

</details>


### [134] [Merging Reaction to Cognition: A Hybrid Cognitive Strategy for Odour Source Localisation in Natural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13853)
*Hugo Magalhães,Rui Baptista,Lino Marques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合策略，将生物启发式反应性行为显式融入认知框架的信念驱动运动规划中，通过检测触发的切换机制在横流探索与源定向运动之间转换，在仿真和实地实验中实现高达50%路径缩短、86%成功率与3.2m平均定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气味源定位方法中，生物启发式策略高效但需场景特定调参，认知策略鲁棒但探索过度且依赖信念精度，两者各有局限，亟需融合两者优势的统一框架。

Method: 提出检测触发的切换机制，在横流探索（crossflow exploration）与源定向运动（source-directed motion）之间转换；行为参数直接从信念度量（belief metrics）中导出，实现无需手动调参的自适应反应性。

Result: 仿真（三种湍流条件）和实地实验（葡萄牙蒙德戈河自主水面航行器）中，旅行距离减少高达50%，成功率86%，平均定位误差3.2m。

Conclusion: 显式引入生物启发式反应性到认知运动规划中能显著提升源定位效率，信念驱动的参数自适应避免了手动调参，为自主机器人气味溯源提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Chemical pollutants released into the environment are transported by turbulent flows, generating complex, intermittent plume structures that threaten ecosystems and human health. Rapid localisation of emission sources is critical, and field robots equipped with chemical sensors provide a viable means to perform this task. However, inferring source location from sensor readings remains difficult due to sparse detections and the absence of reliable concentration gradients. Existing approaches fall into two paradigms. Bio-inspired strategies rely on reactive behaviours triggered by detections, such as surge-casting, offering efficiency but requiring scenario-specific tuning. Cognitive strategies integrate observations into a probabilistic belief over source location. While more robust, they suffer from excessive exploration and strong dependence on belief accuracy. The Fast-Cognitive algorithm reduced this computational burden but preserved the fundamental limitations. Previous Markov chain analysis revealed that source-directed motions occur roughly twice as often following odour detections, indicating that reactive behaviours naturally emerge within cognitive frameworks. This work proposes a hybrid strategy that explicitly incorporates bio-inspired reactivity into belief-dependent motion planning. It introduces a detection-triggered switching mechanism formalising transitions between crossflow exploration and source-directed motion, prioritising source proximity over information gain. Behavioural parameters are derived directly from belief metrics, enabling adaptive reactivity without manual tuning. The approach is validated through simulations under three turbulence conditions and field experiments with an autonomous surface vehicle in the Mondego River, Portugal. Results show up to 50% reduction in travelled distance, 86% success rate, and 3.2m average localisation error.

</details>


### [135] [Learning Forward & Reverse Skills from a Single Unfinished Demonstration for Constrained Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13882)
*Yexin Hu,Haoyi Zheng,Johannes Heidersberger,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的一次性示教框架，用于接触受限的操控任务，将演示分解为无接触和接触阶段，用DMP编码无接触运动，用螺旋运动基元（通过几何驱动的扭转方向分割算法）编码接触运动，在执行中通过导纳引导的位姿修正和速度调节顺序执行螺旋基元，支持超越示教轨迹长度的任务完成和反向技能执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法需要多次完整演示，不支持反向技能执行，难以处理涉及几何约束和力交互的接触密集任务。

Method: 1) 演示分解为非接触和接触阶段；2) 非接触运动用DMP编码；3) 接触运动用几何驱动的扭转方向分割算法分割为螺旋运动基元序列；4) 执行时螺旋基元在导纳控制引导的位姿修正和速度调节下顺序执行。

Result: 在插销、电池插入、开锁和螺丝拧紧任务上，成功率和鲁棒性优于分割和一次性轨迹学习基线，支持反向执行和超越演示长度的操作。

Conclusion: 提出的一次性示教框架有效解决了接触受限任务的示教学习难题，兼具前向和反向执行能力，具有较好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) enables robots to learn manipulation skills directly from expert demonstrations but remains challenging for contact-rich tasks involving geometric constraints and force interaction. Existing approaches typically require multiple complete demonstrations and do not support reverse skill execution. In this paper, we present a unified one-shot framework for constrained manipulation that learns both forward and reverse execution from a single, possibly unfinished demonstration. Our method decomposes demonstrations into non-contact and contact phases, with non-contact motion encoded with dynamic movement primitives (DMP), and contact motion represented as a sequence of screw motion primitives segmented by our proposed geometry-driven twist-direction segmentation algorithm. During execution, screw primitives are executed sequentially under admittance-guided pose correction and speed regulation, enabling task completion beyond the demonstrated trajectory length as well as reverse skill execution without additional learning data. Experiments on peg insertion, battery insertion, lock opening, and screw driving tasks demonstrate improved success rates and robustness over segmentation and one-shot trajectory learning baselines. Details are available on the project website: https://tuwien-asl.github.io/LfD-Screw/.

</details>


### [136] [S-squared-VLA: Decoupling Semantic and Spatial Streams in Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13926)
*Jianguo Yu,Rukang Wang,Duanfeng Chu,Chen Wang,Renju Feng,Liping Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S²-VLA模型，通过解耦语义流和空间流解决VLA中的空间表征坍缩问题，在NAVSIM闭环基准上达到SOTA（PDMS 87.1，无碰撞率98.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将语言视觉与动作统一为单一策略导致空间表征坍缩，丢失细粒度空间先验，无法生成精确的低层控制动作。

Method: 语义流使用分层桥接提取多尺度VLM特征进行意图推理；空间流绕过自回归语言瓶颈直接保留未压缩视觉特征，并辅以感知监督；双流规划适配器通过级联注意力融合高维语义意图与精确空间约束。

Result: NAVSIM闭环基准上PDMS 87.1，无碰撞率98.4，超越所有基线，包括纯SFT设定下的SOTA。

Conclusion: 解耦语义与空间流能有效缓解VLA空间表征坍缩，提升自动驾驶中行为边界感知的安全性与规划精度。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable potential for high-level reasoning in autonomous driving, yet they fundamentally struggle to generate precise, low-level control actions. This limitation is rooted in a semantic-physical gap caused by the inherent mismatch between discrete language tokens and continuous trajectory planning. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures attempt to bridge this gap by unifying perception and control into a single policy, this entanglement creates a new bottleneck. Standard VLAs experience a severe spatial representation collapse, which irreversibly degrades the fine-grained spatial and geometric priors essential for safe, boundary-aware navigation. To address this limitation, we propose the S-squared-VLA, which explicitly decouples the semantic and spatial streams in Vision-Language-Action models. The semantic stream leverages hierarchical bridging to extract multi-scale VLM features for robust intent reasoning. In parallel, an independent spatial stream bypasses the autoregressive language bottleneck, directly preserving uncompressed spatial features from the visual encoder. By integrating auxiliary perception supervision, this stream explicitly equips the model with rich spatial and geometric priors. Finally, a dual-stream planning adapter fuses high-level semantic intent with precise spatial constraints via cascaded attention mechanisms. Evaluations on the NAVSIM closed-loop benchmark show that S-squared-VLA achieves a Predictive Driver Model Score (PDMS) of 87.1, establishing a new state-of-the-art for VLA models under a purely supervised fine-tuning (SFT) setting. By mitigating the spatial representation collapse of traditional VLMs, our framework significantly outperforms baselines, achieving the highest No Collision (NC) rate of 98.4 among all evaluated methods.

</details>


### [137] [Discriminative Barrier Functions for Safe Adversarial Imitation Learning from Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13938)
*Anubhav Vishwakarma,Bhaumik Mehta,Caleb Hsu,Byron Boots,Karen Leung,Tyler Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过将逆强化学习中的奖励函数限制在控制障碍函数空间内，实现安全在线控制并从无标签专家数据中恢复障碍函数，在仿真和真实避障任务中提升了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRL算法虽能从专家演示中学习，但依赖无约束探索导致不安全；CBF可保证控制安全但解析设计耗时。二者局限需要被联合解决。

Method: 将IRL的奖励函数候选空间约束为CBF空间，允许从无标签专家观测中数据驱动地恢复障碍函数，实现安全在线控制与持续经验改进。

Result: 恢复的障碍函数对专家数据中完全未出现的不安全状态具有鲁棒性；在仿真导航环境中优于标准IRL基线；在真实避障任务中验证了规划型与策略型IRL方法的折衷。

Conclusion: 该框架融合了IRL和CBF的优势，实现了数据驱动的安全控制，且恢复的障碍函数具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) algorithms are powerful tools for learning from and generalizing expert demonstrations, but they often rely on unconstrained exploration, rendering them unsafe for real-world deployment. Meanwhile, Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) can guarantee the safety of control systems, but the analytical design of CBFs can be time-consuming and esoteric. In this work, we address these limitations jointly by constraining reward function candidacy during IRL to the space of CBFs, yielding a formulation that exhibits safe online control with continuous experiential improvement. Crucially, this framework enables the data-driven recovery of barrier functions directly from unlabeled expert observations. We demonstrate that the recovered barrier function is robust to unsafe states entirely absent from the expert data. Furthermore, we benchmark our method against standard IRL baselines in a simulated navigation environment, demonstrating improved safety performance. Finally, we investigate the trade-offs of planning-based versus policy-based IRL methods across both simulation and a real world obstacle avoidance task.

</details>


### [138] [GigaWorld-Policy-0.5: A Faster and Stronger WAM Empowered by AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13960)
*GigaWorld Team,Angen Ye,Angyuan Ma,Boyuan Wang,Chaojun Ni,Fangzheng Ye,Guan Huang,Guo Li,Guosheng Zhao,Haodong Yan,Hengtao Li,Jiwen Lu,Kai Wang,Mingming Yu,Qitang Hu,Qiuping Deng,Songling Liu,Xiaoyu Tian,Xiaofeng Wang,Xinyu Zhou,Xiuwei Xu,Xinze Chen,Yang Wang,Yejun Zeng,Yifan Chang,Yun Ye,Zhenyu Wu,Zhanqian Wu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GigaWorld-Policy-0.5提出了一种以动作为中心的世界动作模型（WAM），在训练时使用未来视觉动态作为监督，但在推理时仅解码动作，避免了显式生成未来视频的高计算开销。采用混合训练策略、混合Transformer架构（MoT）分离视觉与动作专家，以及AutoResearch自动调参，在RTX 4090上实现85ms推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在推理时需显式生成未来视频，计算开销大，难以用于实时闭环控制。

Method: 1) 动作中心化公式：训练利用未来视觉动态，推理仅解码动作；2) 混合AC-WM与WAM预训练策略；3) 混合Transformer架构（MoT）分离视觉建模和动作生成专家；4) 基于代理的AutoResearch自动搜索训练配置。

Result: 在本地RTX 4090上实现85ms推理延迟，保持训练收益的同时提升推理效率。

Conclusion: GigaWorld-Policy-0.5验证了动作中心化WAM在保持未来视觉动态训练优势的同时，可大幅降低推理成本，适用于实时机器人控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling actions and future visual observations, using future scene evolution as dense supervision for physically grounded action generation. However, a common design in existing WAMs is to explicitly generate future videos at inference time, incurring substantial computational overhead and hindering real-time closed-loop deployment. GigaWorld-Policy addresses this issue with an action-centered formulation, where future visual dynamics are used during training while action-only decoding is used at inference time. Building upon this framework, we present GigaWorld-Policy-0.5, an enhanced action-centered WAM designed for more efficient robot control. During pretraining, GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 adopts a mixed Action-Conditioned World Modeling (AC-WM) and WAM training strategy. This strengthens the coupling between visual dynamics and robot actions and improves the transferability of action representations for downstream policy learning. For efficient inference, GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 introduces a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that separates visual dynamics modeling and action generation into specialized experts, reducing active computation during action-only inference and achieving 85 ms inference latency on a local RTX 4090 setup. In addition, we employ an agent-based AutoResearch pipeline to systematically search training configurations, enabling more efficient identification of optimal experimental setups while reducing the time and manual intervention required for hyperparameter tuning. Experiments and ablations show that GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 preserves the training benefits of future visual dynamics while improving inference efficiency for robot control.

</details>


### [139] [AeroMap3D: Anchoring Monocular UAV 6-DoF Localization to Visual-Geometric-Semantic Map Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14009)
*Zhiyun Deng,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroMap3D是一种单目6-DoF无人机定位系统，利用视觉、几何和语义地图先验在GNSS拒止环境下实现定位，通过轻量适配器实现无微调的无人机-卫星图像配准，结合DEM和OSM语义信息剔除不可靠匹配，在8个Austin站点上实现5.88米平均3D定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GNSS拒止环境下无人机定位的两个核心挑战：无人机航拍图与卫星地图之间的跨视角差异（交叉视角），以及裸土数字高程模型与城市场景之间的结构不一致性（建筑物高度未建模导致的误差）。

Method: 1) 设计轻量适配器，使预训练密集匹配器无需微调即可实现无人机-地图配准，通过估计尺度比和偏航角消除主要几何不对齐；2) 将2D匹配点提升到DEM地形上，利用OSM标注剔除语义不可靠匹配（如建筑物上的点），再通过RANSAC-PnP进行位姿估计；3) 使用延迟状态EKF融合延迟的地图位姿测量与相对运动先验。

Result: 无需在UAV-Terra3D上重新训练或调参，在8个Austin站点的所有轨迹上定位误差均小于50米，55公里飞行路径上平均3D误差为5.88米。

Conclusion: AeroMap3D通过轻量适配器+语义过滤策略，有效解决了跨视角配准和城市DEM不一致问题，实现了无需重新训练的通用无人机定位系统。

Abstract: We present AeroMap3D, a monocular 6-DoF UAV localization system that anchors onboard imagery to visual, geometric, and semantic map priors for GNSS-denied navigation. AeroMap3D addresses two fundamental challenges in map-referenced aerial localization: the cross-view discrepancy between UAV imagery and satellite maps, and the structural inconsistency between bare-earth digital elevation models (DEMs) and urban scenes. First, we introduce a lightweight adapter that enables a dense matcher pretrained on internet-scale generic data to perform reliable UAV-to-map registration without finetuning. By estimating the scale ratio and yaw offset between the UAV image and map tile, the adapter removes the dominant geometric misalignment induced by altitude, camera field of view, and heading before dense correspondence estimation. Second, AeroMap3D lifts 2D UAV-map correspondences onto DEM terrain while using OpenStreetMap annotations to reject semantically unreliable matches before RANSAC-PnP pose estimation, thereby reducing errors caused by unmodeled building heights and off-nadir structures. Delayed map-based pose measurements are further fused with relative-motion priors using a delayed-state EKF for continuous trajectory estimation. Without UAV-Terra3D retraining or tuning, AeroMap3D localizes all trajectories across eight Austin sites within 50 m and achieves 5.88 m mean 3D error over 55 km of flight.

</details>


### [140] [Industrial Dexterity Benchmark: A Hardware-Software Benchmarking Platform for Industrial Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14021)
*Honglu He,Jacob Laufer,Zhiwu Zheng,David Elkan-gonzalez,Raman Goyal,Xinyi Li,Su Lu,Mishek Musa,Berke Saat,Nicolas Tan,Colm Prendergast*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出从经典模块化机器人管线向端到端多模态模仿学习框架的演进，用于工业灵巧操作。贡献包括：工业灵巧性基准板（IDB）、可扩展模仿学习框架（DAG-ROS）和多模态扩散策略（AG-iDP3）。在数据中心线缆操作任务上，最佳配置（多视图RGB+R3M编码器+多模态扩散策略）达到78%的抓取-插入成功率，远超单相机RGB基线的36%，仅需约100次遥操作演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业灵巧操作（如线缆路由、连接器插拔、精密装配）长期依赖人工，现有机器人方法在鲁棒性、泛化性和部署效率上不足，亟需向可扩展的端到端学习方法转变。

Method: 提出三部分贡献：(1) IDB基准板（模拟数据中心线缆管理、汽车线束、齿轮箱装配）；(2) DAG-ROS可扩展模仿学习框架；(3) AG-iDP3多模态扩散策略，融合RGB图像、点云、关节位置和腕部力/力矩数据。策略基于扩散模型，采用R3M视觉编码器处理多视图RGB输入。

Result: 在数据中心线缆管理板上评估单线缆清洁任务，48次试验/配置。最佳多模态DP（多视图RGB+R3M）达到78%抓取-插入联合成功率，单相机RGB DP基线仅36%。各配置仅需约100次遥操作演示。

Conclusion: 正确的学习策略在鲁棒性、泛化性和部署效率上优于经典视觉与控制方法，证明向可扩展机器人自动化的转变对高可用工业环境是合理的。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation remains a critical bottleneck in industrial automation; tasks such as cable routing, connector insertion, and precision assembly still rely heavily on manual labor despite decades of robotics research. This work presents a progression from classical, modular robotics pipelines toward an end-to-end multimodal imitation-learning framework for industrial dexterous manipulation. As a part of this work, we introduce three key contributions: a set of Industrial Dexterity Benchmark (IDB) boards aimed to mimic datacenter cable management, automotive cable harnesses, and gearbox assembly tasks; a scalable imitation learning framework (DAG-ROS); and a multimodal diffusion-based policy framework (AG-iDP3) that creates models fusing RGB images, point clouds, joint positions, and wrist-frame wrench data. Focusing on the datacenter cable manipulation board, we evaluate the performance of a task involving cleaning a single cable over variations of an end-to-end AI policy using 48 trials per configuration. The best performing configuration, a multimodal expansion Diffusion Policy (DP), includes a multi-view RGB image source passed through an R3M encoder and reaches a 78% grasp and insert combined task success rate. This performance marks a significant improvement over the 36% observed from the single-camera RGB DP baseline. Each of the tested configurations requires only approximately 100 teleoperated demonstrations per task phase. These results indicate that the correct learned policy can outperform classical vision and control robotic methods in robustness, generalization, and deployment efficiency, justifying a shift toward scalable robotic automation for high up-time industrial environments.

</details>


### [141] [PhysClaw-0: A Symbiotic Agentic System for Robot Autonomy via Language Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14047)
*Boyuan Wang,Zhenyuan Zhang,Zhiqin Yang,Peijun Gu,Shuya Wang,Xiaofeng Wang,Xianghui Ze,Yifan Chang,Guosheng Zhao,Jiangnan Shao,Guan Huang,Hengyu Liu,Yonggang Zhang,Wei Xue,Chunyuan Guan,Chenglin Pu,Yike Guo,Xingang Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysClaw-0 是一个人类-机器人共生智能数据采集系统，通过将语言矫正存入“矫正记忆”实现跨回合复用，在桌面清理任务中将人类工作时间降至16%，同时保持与遥操作相当的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主数据采集管线中，每当相同失败重复出现就必须重新下发回合级修正指令，人力开销随会话长度而非不同问题数量线性增长。需要一种能保留并复用过去修正的机制来降低长期采集的人力成本。

Method: 构建一个自主采集循环（采集→验证→重置），仅在重试预算耗尽时暂停请求远程操作员介入；操作员的自然语言修正经 LLM 解析后存入结构化矫正记忆（Corrective Memory），后续相同条件下自动复用已有修正而非重新请求。

Result: 在真实机器人桌面清理测试中，人类工作时间降至16%；语言修正将验证器-人类一致性提升，单次尝试成功率从12.5%提升至47.5%（臂选择从20.0%到50.0%）；微调策略达到与遥操作训练策略相当的成功率。

Conclusion: 矫正记忆机制可显著降低长期数据采集的人力成本，同时保持或提升数据质量与策略性能，为大规模机器人数据采集提供了一种更可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Autonomous data collection governs the volume and quality of real-world trajectories for manipulation policy learning. Existing pipelines reduce human effort via self-resetting, VLM verification, or language-guided correction, yet episode-scoped fixes must be reissued whenever the same failure recurs, so oversight cost grows with session length rather than with the number of distinct problems. We present PhysClaw-0, a human-robot symbiotic agentic system in which corrections are retained and reused across rounds. The collection loop collects, verifies, and resets autonomously, pausing for a remote operator only when a phase exhausts an explicit retry budget. An LLM parser maps each natural-language utterance to a structured adjustment stored in Corrective Memory, so addressed failure modes typically need not be corrected again under the same conditions. On a real-robot desktop-clearing testbed, PhysClaw-0 matches teleoperation episode success while reducing human working time to 16%. Language corrections improve verifier-human agreement in all four evaluated settings and raise average single-attempt success from 12.5% to 47.5% (arm-selection: 20.0% to 50.0%). Policies fine-tuned on PhysClaw-0 data match teleoperation-trained policy success at a fraction of collection human cost.

</details>
